# The Tide Mother — Image Prompts for Grok Imagine (short clips, one image per clip)

*172 beats, 436 clips, 31:03 total. Every clip is **3–6 seconds** and starts from **its own image**, so faces, costumes and voices never drift. Conversations are shot **from behind the listener**: the listener's back is in the foreground and the speaker faces the camera. Nights are truly dark, lit only by four fixed medieval light sources. Visuals follow the original FABLEON Visual Style Profile.*

**How to use:** generate every clip image with the character sheets and the location sheet attached as references. Never chain clips from the last frame of the previous clip; always start each clip from its own image.

## PROLOGUE — THE LAW OF KELVHAVEN


### Prologue — Kelvhaven


**Beat 001 · 0:00–0:06 · 6s · Opening hook · 1 clip**

- **Script segment:** "A storm-black sea. Somewhere beneath it, something opens its eye."
- **Season:** Start of winter (the great storm): cold rain, wind and sea spray; no snow
- **Location sheet:** none (open sea or underwater)
- **Clip 001-1 image prompt:** Photorealistic cinematic film still, dark fantasy, shot on 35mm, fine film grain, high-fidelity textures, shallow depth of field, natural bokeh, desaturated high-contrast color grade, 16:9. Extreme close-up, underwater: a single huge luminous cyan slit-pupil eye opening in pitch-black water, faint bioluminescent blue veins around it. The moment in this clip: Black water, only drifting particles. Setting: deep underwater in a cold northern sea, drifting particles, kelp shadows, light fading from the surface far above. Lighting: darkness pierced by electric-blue bioluminescent glow. Mood: ominous, mysterious. Palette: storm slate grey, fog grey, driftwood brown and wet-wool grey. No text, no watermark, no modern objects.

### Cave mouth — the mob


**Beat 108 · 18:48–19:03 · 15s · Major climax · 3 clips**

- **Script segment:** "Low tide. The sea pulls back, and the cave lies open. Torches. Dozens of them. The villagers push inside. And behind Maren, Tal rises. He is as big as a full-grown horse now."
- **Season:** Early winter chill: cold wind, grey skies, drizzle and sea mist; no snow, no ice
- **Location sheet:** L11 The hidden cove
- **Clip 108-1 image prompt:** Photorealistic cinematic film still, dark fantasy, shot on 35mm, fine film grain, desaturated high-contrast color grade, 16:9. REAR VIEW. High drone shot from behind and above a running mob, looking forward over their heads along a long dark black-sand beach toward a sea cave far away. This is a rear tracking shot: the camera is BEHIND the mob, so we see ONLY THE BACKS of the villagers. LOWER HALF OF THE FRAME (closest to the camera): about twenty-five villagers running AWAY from the camera, toward the top of the frame. We see the backs of their heads, raised hoods, the backs of their cloaks and coats, and the soles of their boots lifting as they run. No villager's face is visible. Their cloaks and the smoke of their pitch torches stream BACKWARD toward the camera from the speed of running. They hold the pitch torches raised high and forward, at uneven heights, so the orange light falls on the wet sand IN FRONT of them, and their backs are dark silhouettes rimmed with orange light. A long trail of fresh footprints in the wet sand runs from the bottom edge of the frame up to the mob. At the very front, the broadest back of all: a big man in a heavy black fleece-lined coat with a shaggy black fleece collar, an iron harpoon held up in his right hand. UPPER THIRD OF THE FRAME (about 70 meters away, small because of the distance): the dark mouth of a sea cave at the far end of the beach. In front of it stands a lone woman in a long black wool kirtle and black hooded mantle, FACING THE CAMERA and the oncoming mob, arms spread. Just behind her shoulder stands a horse-sized sea dragon, his head level with hers, gunmetal-grey iridescent scales, faint glowing cyan eyes and blue veins. These two are the only faces in the image, small and far away. Between the mob and the cave: 70 meters of empty dark wet sand reflecting the torchlight. TRUE NIGHT: deep darkness, black sky, a thin moon behind cloud. The only light is the moving orange torchlight around the mob and the faint cyan glow at the far cave. Early winter chill, drizzle, sea mist, no snow, no ice. Palette: storm slate grey, fog grey, driftwood brown and wet-wool grey, with orange pitch torches against cold blue darkness. No faces on the villagers, no one looking at the camera, no text, no watermark, no modern objects. Fixed medieval light sources, same design in every scene: pitch torches are wooden staves with rag heads soaked in fish oil and pitch.
